### Step 5: Rewire megaplan — single helper for shim+import+error-conversion, filesystem availability checks, `agent_deps_missing` UX, supporting infrastructure (`megaplan/*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper to `megaplan/hermes_worker.py`** (near the other module-private helpers, above `run_hermes_step`):
   ```python
   def _import_hermes_runtime():
       """Trigger the sys.path shim and return (AIAgent, SessionDB).

       All hermes execution sites route through this helper so that the
       `[agent]`-extras-missing case produces a uniform actionable error
       message. Do NOT import `run_agent` / `hermes_state` directly from
       megaplan code outside this function — the AST gate in Step 9.1
       enforces this.
       """
       import megaplan.agent  # noqa: F401 — sys.path shim
       try:
           from run_agent import AIAgent
           from hermes_state import SessionDB
       except ImportError as exc:
           from megaplan.types import CliError
           raise CliError(
               "agent_deps_missing",
               "hermes backend requires: pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]'",
           ) from exc
       return AIAgent, SessionDB
   ```
2. **Replace direct hermes imports with helper calls at all FOUR execution sites:**
   - **`megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290-291`** in `run_hermes_step`: replace `from run_agent import AIAgent` / `from hermes_state import SessionDB` with `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:80-81`** in `_run_check`: same pattern — `from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime` at function top (inside the function body), then `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:209-210`** in `_run_criteria_verdict`: same pattern as above.
   - **`megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51-52`** in `_run_check`: same pattern.

   ```python
       if agent == "hermes":

           return (Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file()
       return bool(shutil.which(agent))
   ```

5. **Delete `check_hermes_available()`** from `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:23-49` along with its `from hermes_cli.config import get_env_path` usage.
6. **Rewrite gating in `megaplan/workers.py:1643-1658`** — replace both `check_hermes_available()` call sites with inline branches that raise `CliError("agent_deps_missing", ...)` when explicit hermes selection meets `not _is_agent_available("hermes")` (same code as iter 5 — keeps the actionable error for the case where vendored files are genuinely absent, e.g. git-clone-without-subtree).
7. **Supporting infrastructure:**
   - `megaplan/key_pool.py:62-74` — drop the `repo_root.parent / "hermes-agent" / ...` sibling-repo fallback.
   - `megaplan/workers.py:1662-1665` — change text to `"Install claude or codex, or pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]' for hermes."`.

### Step 6: Tests — no changes required
**Scope:** Trivial
1. `tests/test_parallel_critique.py:404-407` and `tests/test_parallel_review.py:198-201, 281-284` keep their `monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "run_agent", ...)` / `sys.modules["hermes_state"]` fake pattern. The helper does `from run_agent import AIAgent; from hermes_state import SessionDB` — Python resolves `sys.modules` before sys.path, so the fakes intercept and the helper returns (FakeAIAgent, FakeSessionDB).
2. Confirmation: run `pytest tests/test_parallel_critique.py tests/test_parallel_review.py -q` after Step 5 and verify green.

### Step 7: Delete dead weight (`git rm` inside `megaplan/agent/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. `git rm -r` brief's list minus `hermes_cli/` and any Step 3.3 retentions:
   ```
   evals/ landingpage/ website/ demo/ assets/ node_modules/
   package.json package-lock.json
   tinker-atropos/ mini-swe-agent/
   cli.py auto_improve/ batch_runner.py rl_cli.py
   datagen-config-examples/
   RELEASE_v*.md setup-hermes.sh cli-config.yaml.example
   ```
   For `gateway/`, `cron/`, `honcho_integration/`: delete ONLY if Step 3.3 confirms references are try/except-guarded or unreachable.
2. Root-level `*.json` benchmark artifacts: `git ls-files 'megaplan/agent/*.json'`, delete obvious benchmark outputs only.
3. Document retentions (if any) in commit body.

### Step 8: Packaging — `[agent]` extra (`pyproject.toml`, `README.md`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Harvest runtime deps verbatim from `megaplan/agent/pyproject.toml`.
2. Add `[project.optional-dependencies] agent = [...]` to `megaplan/pyproject.toml`. Baseline `dependencies` stays `["PyYAML>=6.0"]`.
3. Confirm `[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]` includes `megaplan/agent/`. Exclude the nested `megaplan/agent/pyproject.toml` from the wheel if hatch complains.
4. README line 23 → `pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]'`; one-liner about the `[agent]` extra.

### Step 9: Verify (`bash`, `pytest`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cheap grep gates:**
   - `grep -r "check_hermes_available" megaplan/ tests/` → zero.
   - `grep -n "hermes-agent" megaplan/key_pool.py` → zero.
   - **Positive AST gate (addresses all_locations, all_locations-1, correctness-3 by construction):** script that parses every `megaplan/**/*.py` outside `megaplan/agent/` with `ast` and asserts: (a) zero module-top `Import`/`ImportFrom` targets `megaplan.agent`; (b) zero `ImportFrom` statements with `module in {"run_agent", "hermes_state"}` anywhere EXCEPT inside the function `_import_hermes_runtime` in `megaplan/hermes_worker.py`; (c) `_import_hermes_runtime` body contains a `Try` whose body has `ImportFrom(module="run_agent")` and `ImportFrom(module="hermes_state")` and whose `except ImportError` handler raises a `Call` to `CliError` with first arg literal `"agent_deps_missing"`. Emit pass/fail with the offending file:line for any violation.
2. **Strengthened isolation probe:**
   ```
   python -c "
   import sys
   before = list(sys.path)
   import megaplan
   from megaplan.workers import _is_agent_available
   from megaplan._core.io import detect_available_agents
   _ = _is_agent_available('claude')
   _ = _is_agent_available('codex')
   _ = _is_agent_available('hermes')
   _ = detect_available_agents()
   after = list(sys.path)
   assert before == after, f'sys.path mutated: before={before}, after={after}'
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Must exit 0 without hermes runtime deps installed.
3. **Bare-Python megaplan import probe** (no hermes deps):
   ```
   python -c "import megaplan.hermes_worker, megaplan.workers, megaplan.review.parallel, megaplan.parallel_critique, megaplan._core.io, megaplan.key_pool"
   ```
4. **Hermetic shim-coverage probe** (venv with `pip install -e '.[agent]'`):
   ```
   HOME=$(mktemp -d) python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()
   import model_tools
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Exercises the helper (which triggers the shim) + dynamic `tools.*` loader via `model_tools` module-scope imports.
5. **`agent_deps_missing` end-to-end for ALL four execution paths** (venv WITHOUT `[agent]`):
   ```
   python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   try:
       _import_hermes_runtime()
       raise SystemExit('expected CliError')
   except Exception as exc:
       assert exc.__class__.__name__ == 'CliError' and 'agent_deps_missing' in str(exc.code), exc
       print('OK: helper raises agent_deps_missing')
   "
   ```
   Since all four execution sites call the helper, verifying the helper covers all four paths. Additionally, smoke-test one real CLI path: `megaplan --agent hermes <args>` must produce `agent_deps_missing`.
6. **Targeted pytest:** `pytest tests/test_parallel_critique.py tests/test_parallel_review.py -q`.
7. **Full suite:** `pytest tests/ -q`.
8. **CLI smoke + isolation:** `megaplan --help` exit 0; re-run probe 9.2 afterward.

## Execution Order
1. Clean tree + snapshot hermes HEAD (Step 1).
2. Subtree merge (Step 2).
3. Diagnostic enumeration + gateway/cron/honcho audit + execution-site final count (Step 3).
4. Install sys.path shim (Step 4).
5. Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper + route all four sites through it + filesystem availability checks + delete `check_hermes_available` + `agent_deps_missing` CliError branch in `workers.resolve_agent_mode` + key_pool/workers text fixes (Step 5).
6. Confirm tests pass unchanged (Step 6).
7. Delete dead weight (Step 7), honoring Step 3.3 retentions.
8. Packaging + README (Step 8).
9. Validate (Step 9).

## Validation Order
1. Cheap greps + positive AST gate (proves by construction that no direct hermes import exists outside the helper).
2. Strengthened isolation probe (sys.path unchanged after availability calls).
3. Bare-Python megaplan import probe.
4. Hermetic helper+dynamic-loader probe under `HOME=$(mktemp -d)`.
5. `agent_deps_missing` probe on the helper (covers all four sites since all route through it) + one CLI smoke.
6. Targeted pytest.
7. Full pytest.
8. `megaplan --help` + re-verify isolation.
9. (info) Manual hermes-backed phase run after `[agent]` install.

        Known accepted debt grouped by subsystem:
        {
  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-009",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the mobile action-surface plan is technically incorrect for the current component structure. `submissioncarouselcard` hides `footercontent` below the `md` breakpoint (`src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx:219`), while `submissionscarouselpage` also hides the score panel on mobile via `asidecontent={<div classname=\"hidden md:block\">...` and passes `hideactions` to `scorepanel` at `src/pages/submissionscarouselpage.tsx:250-263,347`. reusing that pattern for entrypage would leave mobile users with no navigation controls and no scoring controls.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "move-the-entry-page-into-20260401-0450"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-010",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: i checked the sign-in callback path again. `submissionslayout` already wires `usescoring(..., { onrequiresignin: () => pagemachine.dispatch({ type: 'open_sign_in_modal' }) })` at `src/pages/submissionslayout.tsx:38-40`, and `scorepanel` still has its own `onrequiresignin` prop. the revised step 4.2 is acceptable only if it explicitly rewires that prop to `pagemachine.dispatch`; otherwise removing local modal state would orphan the direct ui callback. the plan hints at this, but it remains easy to misread because the scoring-context callback and the scorepanel prop are separate mechanisms.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "move-the-entry-page-into-20260401-0450"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-029",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new diff-helper proposal against the repo's existing execution-evidence semantics. `validate_execution_evidence()` in `megaplan/evaluation.py:109-175` uses `git status --short`, so untracked files count as real changed files today, but step 3 proposes `collect_git_diff_patch(project_dir)` via `git diff head`; by git behavior that will not include untracked files, so heavy review could miss exactly the newly created files that execute and review already treat as part of the patch.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "robust-review-v2"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-032",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: a generated-schema gap remains. `tests/test_schemas.py` reads the repo-root `.megaplan/schemas/review.json` directly via `_review_disk_schema()`, the checked-in local file is currently stale relative to `schemas`, and `ensure_runtime_layout()` in `megaplan/_core/io.py` is the production writer for that file class. the plan says generated schemas must stay aligned, but it never names a regeneration step or a test adjustment for that disk-schema path, so the validation story is still incomplete.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-6-remaining-openai-20260409-0502"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-046",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new pre-extraction step that migrates `pinnedshotgroups` out of core is still under-specified for compatibility. current runtime and test code reads `config.pinnedshotgroups` directly across `hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts`, `lib/pinned-group-projection.ts`, `hooks/useshotgroups.ts`, `components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx`, `hooks/usetimelinetrackmanagement.ts`, `lib/serialize.test.ts`, `lib/migrate.test.ts`, and many other tests, so landing that migration before extraction needs a dual-read/projection shim or a clearly synchronized update plan instead of only a schema/serializer note.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-plan-to-extract-the-20260419-1637"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-050",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: runner inputs: the revised auto-mode snippet calls `$(cat ${idea_file})` and the chain-mode path runs `mp-chain ${chain_spec}`, but step 7's deploy flow still only does `railway link`, `railway variables --set`, and `railway up`. there is no corresponding step that copies the idea file or chain spec into `/workspace`, so a literal implementation would boot into auto/chain mode and immediately fail on missing input files.",

      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: i checked the skip caller path against the real hook behavior and `removefromqueue(entryid)` looks redundant. `scoring.skipentry(entryid)` optimistically adds the id to `skippedentryids` in `src/hooks/usescoring.ts:245-249`, and `usecarouselqueue` already reactively filters skipped entries from `submissions[]` in `src/hooks/usecarouselqueue.ts:154-165`. calling `removefromqueue()` immediately afterward is safe, but it duplicates an existing state transition and should be justified as an explicit ux optimization rather than required logic.",

        Robustness level:

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

PROCEED with accept_tradeoff for the four same-concern flags.
